[04:16:40 +18768.4s]       [prop-reach] ⇒ STANDING VERDICT: CLOSED (19bq). Both open projector ideas (the unreachable prop-keys AND the 899k back-fill rows) reduce to this one number, and it is too small to be worth the risk described next. [04:16:40 +18768.4s]       [prop-reach]    THE RISK THAT MAKES IT NOT FREE: a dead row can still feed a CELL-level denominator (psum / vpsum / ctot) that a LIVE row in the same cell divides by. Dropping it would then move that denominator and the answer with it. What would settle it: build the reduced scaffold, run the projector on both, and compare bit patterns — the same restricted-vs-full test that proved the blocked-caps and eligibility reductions. Do NOT adopt on this count alone.
